Result: 在真实数据集上，该方法在分类和降维任务中表现出色，有效保留了显著拓扑特征。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图向量化为EPD提供了一种高效、自适应的离散化方案，在不依赖光滑模型的前提下实现稳定的拓扑特征表示。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对holonomy-cover决策过程，构建了稳定商（stable quotient）这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量，并证明观测与稳定类组合构成精确有限马尔可夫状态。在可重置诊断下，近邻原型类推理具有指数衰减误差。实验从原始状态恢复精确压缩，以三个决策时记忆状态达到完美配对顺序精度，超越非神谕基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中，智能体需要维护一个可递归更新的历史统计量来恢复马尔可夫性质，但最小这样的统计量通常是未知的。本文旨在为holonomy-cover决策过程这一结构化POMDP类刻画最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 构建稳定商（stable quotient）——最粗的观测层面抽象，保持一步奖励和商后继；证明观测与稳定类对构成精确有限马尔可夫状态。在可重置诊断下，采用近邻原型类推理实现指数衰减误差。提出校准-重启归约将有限MDP保证转移到恢复状态。

Result: 实验从原始状态恢复精确压缩到商状态，以三个决策时记忆状态达到完美配对顺序精度，匹配商神谕并优于非神谕基线。

Conclusion: 本文为holonomy-cover决策过程提供了最小马尔可夫充分统计量的刻画，并实现了Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，在实验中取得了理想结果。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WindCastNet，首个基于卫星散射计观测的海上风场短时临近预报框架。通过部分卷积长短期记忆网络处理多源散射计（欧洲、中国、印度）的不规则时空观测数据，在北海区域评估中，1小时和2小时提前期的RMSE相比HARMONIE MEPS分别降低23%和7%，前3小时预报优于持续性预报9-15%。结果表明卫星散射计星座可为短期海上风电预报提供独立且有竞争力的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风电的分钟-小时级准确预报对电力系统运行日益重要，但传统数值天气预报（NWP）在短提前期（初始条件精度主导预报技巧）并未优化；卫星散射计观测虽常规同化至NWP，却从未被直接用于预报。因此需要探索利用卫星散射计观测直接进行海上风场短时临近预报的新方法。

Method: 采用部分卷积长短期记忆（Partial Convolutional LSTM）网络，处理欧洲、中国、印度散射计星座的微波雷达观测数据，编码空间观测掩码和观测间隔，利用连续时间表示实现任意提前期的预报，从而克服散射计数据空间覆盖不规则、采样异步、重访时间可变等挑战。

Result: 在北海区域，WindCastNet在1小时和2小时提前期相比HARMONIE MEPS的RMSE分别降低23%和7%；前3小时预报优于持续性预报9-15%；强风条件和空间非均匀流场下预报技巧下降。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座能够为短期海上风电预报提供独立且具有竞争力的数据源，开创了基于卫星的短时临近预报新范式，不仅促进可再生能源预报发展，还可拓展至热带气旋临近预报等更广泛的海洋气象应用。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [82] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARC方法，将连续代数约束系统转化为因子图，利用图神经扩散去噪器生成赋值、计算机代数能量精化、符号验证器确认解。研究表明，在相同精化预算下，学习型提案相比随机多起点搜索的优势仅在特定高维场景中存在，且被经典多起点方法全面超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续代数约束系统的求解面临两个决策：找出满足约束的值，以及通过结构增广使不可解系统变得可解。经典方法能很好地回答第一个问题，但第二个问题只能通过枚举解决。

Method: MARC将约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经扩散去噪器生成候选赋值，通过精确的计算机代数能量进行下降精化，最后由符号验证器确认解的正确性。实验系统性地比较了学习型提案与随机多起点方法在相同精化预算下的表现。

Result: 在候选条件化修复排序器中，K种增广方案以远少于穷举搜索的调用次数达到穷举搜索上限（平衡非线性菜单准确率0.997 vs 0.236；p<10^-70；跨种子0.982±0.006）。学习型提案仅在维度升高、随机搜索失效的高维场景中略占优势；变量耦合后优势消失。经典多起点方法成功解决全部8个真实世界系统，无一处于学习型提案的有利区间。

Conclusion: 本文绘制了学习型提案改进求解器的适用区间，指出其优势仅限于特定高维无耦合场景，在真实系统中古典多起点方法仍是最优选择。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [83] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现期权定价精度不等于隐含风险中性密度恢复精度，通过合成基准和NIFTY实盘数据对比多种方法，证明存在目标依赖的归纳偏置而非通用最优方法。条件数分析揭示定价映射存在大量数值零空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示期权定价准确性与风险中性密度恢复准确性之间的本质区别，避免在实践中错误地用定价误差代理密度估计质量。

Method: 构建两个互补基准：受控合成基准（已知真实密度）和NIFTY实盘数据基准。对比双组分对数正态混合、DeepONet、报价变换器等多种方法，并进行数值条件数分析。

Result: 双组分对数正态混合在合成基准上综合最优；DeepONet在分位数和方差误差上更优；报价变换器在Merton族上L1误差降低16.4%。条件数分析显示95/126个定价方向数值为零，L1=0.061的两个密度产生相同价格。测试时适应可将DeepONet RMSE降低28.3%，但混合和SVI拟合仍更准确。

Conclusion: 不同密度恢复目标需要不同的归纳偏置，不存在通用的最优方法。密度恢复问题需要针对具体任务选择合适的方法。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [84] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文受果蝇感知机制的启发，提出了一种基于分类的回归方法，通过构建局部代表性模式库来学习非线性输入输出关系，并应用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归任务中，复杂全局代理模型计算和存储成本高；受果蝇感知环境机制的启发，希望用有限局部模式库替代全局模型，以降低计算和内存需求，并实现精度、存储与推理成本之间的显式权衡。

Method: 提出通用框架：构建有限库存储代表性局部模式；对查询点，通过相似度度量与存储模式比较，再对关联响应进行加权重构以生成预测。针对动力系统，采用离线-在线工作流，离线阶段从数据或控制方程提取模式，在线阶段仅需相似度评估和响应聚合。

Result: 该方法在非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习任务中均有效，能够降低计算与内存需求，并提供精度-存储-推理成本之间的显式可控权衡。

Conclusion: 基于分类的局部模式库方法是一种有效替代全局代理模型的学习范式，适用于多种科学计算任务，具有计算高效和可控性强的优势。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [85] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于价值函数的强化学习中，预训练Q函数是否有助于微调性能。发现朴素Q函数预训练收益有限，根本原因在于预训练阶段学习的Q函数与在线微调收敛的Q函数之间存在不匹配。为此提出初始化策略集成（IPE）方法，通过训练多个多样化策略并利用其混合轨迹引导Q函数学习，在连续控制基准上取得平均1.26倍的微调性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于价值的强化学习中，预训练策略后是否应该同时预训练Q函数？传统观点认为应该，但近期研究表明随机初始化的Q函数在线微调即可获得高性能策略。本文旨在系统研究预训练Q函数对微调性能的实际影响及其根本原因。

Method: 提出初始化策略集成（IPE）方法：训练多个多样化策略，收集它们的混合轨迹数据，用这些数据来引导在线RL中的Q函数初始化学习。

Result: 在连续控制基准任务上，IPE相比朴素Q函数预训练平均提升1.26倍的微调性能。

Conclusion: 朴素Q函数预训练收益有限源于预训练目标与在线微调目标之间的函数不匹配问题。IPE通过集成多样化策略的混合数据缓解了这一不匹配。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [When Kernel Ridge Regression Meets the Hölder-Zygmund Class: Minimax Optimality and Failure of Properness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26065)
*Yuxuan Hou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Hölder-Zygmund类上进行非参数回归的核岭回归（KRR）方法。通过使用与光滑度为s+d/2的Sobolev空间等价的RKHS，证明了错误设定的KRR能够达到极小极大L2速率n^{-2s/(2s+d)}。同时，在Hölder-Zygmund范数下，即使对于带高斯噪声的零回归函数，KRR噪声分量的期望平方Hölder-Zygmund范数以log n增长，表明properness性质不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核岭回归在Hölder-Zygmund类上的表现，特别是当核函数与真实函数类不匹配（misspecified）时的收敛性质，以及KRR在不同范数下的表现。

Method: 使用与光滑度为s+d/2的Sobolev空间等价的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS），对Hölder-Zygmund类的非参数回归问题进行核岭回归分析，分别从L2范数和Hölder-Zygmund范数两个角度考察估计性能。

Result: 1) 错误设定的KRR在L2范数下达到极小极大最优速率n^{-2s/(2s+d)}；2) 在Hölder-Zygmund范数下，对于零回归函数加高斯噪声的简单情形，KRR噪声分量的期望平方Hölder-Zygmund范数以log n发散。

Conclusion: KRR在L2范数下对Hölder-Zygmund类具有自适应的极小极大最优性，但在更强的Hölder-Zygmund范数下不具备properness性质，即估计量的正则性不如真实函数。

Abstract: We study kernel ridge regression for nonparametric regression over the Hölder-Zygmund class. Using an RKHS equivalent to a Sobolev space of smoothness s+d/2, we prove that misspecified KRR attains the minimax L2 rate n^{-2s/(2s+d)}. We also show that properness fails in the Hölder-Zygmund norm: even for the zero regression function with Gaussian noise, the expected squared Hölder-Zygmund norm of the KRR noise component grows as log n.

</details>


### [87] [Origins and mitigation of double descent in reduced order modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26414)
*Andrei A. Klishin,J. Nathan Kutz,Krithika Manohar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的数据-噪声平均理论，解释稀疏感知中双下降现象的形成机制，提供定量预测和正则化缓解方法，并在SST重建和PDE降阶模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然和工程系统数据集中的潜在低维结构使其能够通过少量精心选择的局部测量实现稀疏感知（即全状态重建）。然而，重建风险曲线在不同算法、传感器位置和测量噪声下表现出多样模式，包括机器学习文献中著名的双下降（误差急剧峰值）现象。现有研究缺乏对这些现象的统一理论解释。

Method: 提出统一的数据-噪声平均理论（Data-Noise Averaging theory）：定性上建立了双下降出现的充分条件，归因于重建中病态信号的灾难性放大；定量上以极低计算代价预测详细风险曲线，追踪单个传感器及其组合导致的重建不稳定性，并提供正则化机制来缓解不稳定性。

Result: 在海表温度（SST）模式的静态重建和PDE降阶模型的时间积分中均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 实现了对稀疏感知中双下降现象的定性和定量理解，能够以极低计算成本预测风险曲线、定位不稳定传感器，并通过正则化有效缓解不稳定性。

Abstract: Latent low-dimensional structure in datasets of natural and engineered systems enables their sparse sensing, or full-state reconstruction from historical data and very few carefully chosen localized measurements. Depending on the reconstruction algorithm, sensor locations, and measurement noise, the reconstruction risk curves demonstrate a diversity of patterns including a dramatic peak in error known as double descent in Machine Learning literature. Here we explore those scenarios under a unified Data-Noise Averaging theory. Qualitatively, we formulate sufficient criteria for double descent to emerge through a catastrophic amplification of a pathological signal in reconstruction. Quantitatively, we predict the detailed risk curves at a fraction of computational cost, trace reconstruction instability to individual sensors and their combinations, and provide regularization mechanisms to mitigate the instability. We demonstrate results for both static reconstruction of Sea Surface Temperature patterns and time integration of a reduced order model of a PDE.

</details>


### [88] [Chaos Is a LADDER: Domain Generalization Beyond Invariance via Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26458)
*Yuhang Jiang,Fengchuan Zhang,Sanguo Zhang,Guojun Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出LADDER方法，通过解耦因果/风格表征、冻结编码器、训练源域特定分类器，并在推理时仅用未标注目标域协变量计算权重来组合这些分类器，实现领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域泛化方法追求因果表征的跨域不变性，但当领域本身调节因果内容到响应的映射时，这种不变性假设过于严格。直接将领域风格输入预测器会产生误导性捷径，因为风格本身不直接导致响应。

Method: LADDER包含四个步骤：1) 学习因果表征和风格表征的解耦；2) 冻结编码器；3) 为每个源域训练独立的分类器；4) 推理时仅利用未标注的目标域协变量集，计算各源域分类器的权重（无需目标标签或模型状态更新）。

Result: 在模拟数据、FMoW数据集和基于位置分组的iWildCam协议上验证，在总体精度和分组平均精度上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 风格信息可以定位未见目标域在源域中的位置，指导哪些依赖于领域的预测规则更可信，从而利用而非忽视多源风格的多样性。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to learn from multiple source domains and generalize to unseen target domains. Most DG methods pursue invariance: they seek a causal representation whose prediction rule is invariant across domains. This principle is effective when the causal mechanism is stable, but becomes restrictive when the domain itself modulates how causal content maps to the response. In this case, directly feeding domain style into the predictor can create misleading shortcuts, since style does not by itself cause the response. Yet the apparent chaos of multiple styles can become a ladder: style can locate the unseen target domain among source domains and guide which domain-dependent prediction rules should be trusted. We propose \emph{Latent Adaptive Domain Disentanglement and Environment Reweighting} (LADDER), a fixed-model DG pipeline that learns causal/style representations, freezes the encoders, fits source-specific classifiers, and uses an unlabeled target-domain covariate set only at inference to compute weights over these fixed classifiers, with no target labels or model-state updates. We establish theoretical guarantees for source reweighting and validate LADDER on simulations, FMoW, and a location-grouped iWildCam protocol, with gains in overall and group-averaged accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [Early Failure Prediction from Near-Anomaly Detection: A Proactive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26704)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Christoffel函数的无监督异常预判方法CANARI，用于检测接近决策边界的"近异常"样本，在工业电路板测试数据上优于双阈值基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法对靠近分布边界的样本行为不确定，无法预判未来可能发生的异常，缺乏主动预警能力。

Method: 提出CANARI方法，利用Christoffel函数的强大理论基础，在无监督条件下检测"近异常"样本——即尚未异常但接近边界且可能在近期转变为异常的样本。

Result: 在印刷电路板工业在线测试数据上，通过合成近异常样本进行验证，CANARI优于采用双阈值机制（分别检测异常和近异常）的基线方法。

Conclusion: CANARI提供了一种在异常发生前进行预判的主动式解决方案，为弹性系统、预测性维护和质量控制提供了有前景的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection methods often have uncertain behavior with respect to samples near the distribution boundary, limiting their ability to anticipate future anomalies. This work introduces the concept of near-anomalies that, while not yet anomalous, lie close to the boundary and are likely to transition into anomalies in the near future. To address this, we propose an unsupervised method, named Christoffel-based ANomaly Anticipation for eaRly dIscovery (CANARI), which leverages the strong theoretical foundations of the Christoffel function to detect near-anomalies. The method is validated on industrial in-circuit testing data from printed circuit boards, with synthetically generated near-anomaly samples due to the lack of real-world data labeling. Experimental results show that CANARI outperforms the compared baselines that generally use a dual-threshold mechanism (one for anomalies and one for near-anomalies). It therefore provides a proactive solution for anticipating anomalies before they occur, offering a promising approach for resilience, predictive maintenance, and quality control.

</details>


### [90] [Crossing-Free Probabilistic K-Line Forecasts Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26792)
*Runyao Yu,Yuchen Tao,Yujie Chen,Wentao Wang,Derek W. Bunn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出KQSP方法，一种无参数、无需重新训练的协调方法，能够同时解决概率K线预测中的分位数交叉和K线交叉两个一致性问题，在不损失预测精度的前提下将交叉率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率K线预测中存在两个一致性问题：分位数交叉（高分位数预测低于低分位数预测）和K线交叉（预测低点高于开盘/收盘价，或预测高点低于开盘/收盘价）。现有方法通常只能解决其中一个问题，且依赖于输出重排序、特殊架构或惩罚训练目标。

Method: 提出K线-分位数序列投影（KQSP）方法，这是一种无参数、无需训练的协调方法，可适用于任意模型生成的预测结果。通过序列投影机制同时保证分位数一致性和K线一致性。

Result: KQSP将分位数交叉率和K线交叉率均降至零；相比其他交叉问题解决方案，KQSP在保持预测精度的同时，对原始预测的修正幅度更小。

Conclusion: 概率K线一致性可以独立于预测生成过程实现，无需重新训练模型即可强制执行一致性约束。

Abstract: Probabilistic K-line forecasting describes uncertainty in four complementary prices, namely open--high--low--close (OHLC). However, it introduces two consistency problems: quantile crossing and K-line crossing. Quantile crossing occurs when a higher-quantile forecast falls below a lower-quantile forecast, while K-line crossing occurs when the forecast low exceeds the open or close, or the forecast high falls below the open or close. Existing solutions generally address only one problem through output reordering, specialized architectures, or penalized training objectives. We propose K-line--Quantile Sequential Projection (KQSP), a parameter-free and training-free reconciliation method applicable to forecasts produced by any model. Compared with other crossing solutions, KQSP preserves predictive accuracy while producing substantially smaller corrections to the original forecasts. To mitigate model bias, we evaluate KQSP using various models, including pretrained foundation models. KQSP reduces both quantile and K-line crossing rates to zero for all test data undertaken. These results show that probabilistic K-line consistency can be enforced independently of forecast generation and without retraining.

</details>


### [91] [Think Short, Defer Smart, Act, and Repeat: Calibrated Reasoning and Uncertainty-Aware Deferral for Edge LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26865)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出TSDS框架，通过轻量级收敛探针（提前停止思考）和困惑度-based延迟规则（将不确定动作交给云端），结合多目标Learn-Then-Test校准，在四个ReAct基准上实现43%-73%的推理计算缩减，同时保证奖励和云端调用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署的LLM agent需要在有限推理预算下保持可靠性，当本地不确定性过高时安全地转交给云端模型，现有方法缺乏同时控制计算成本和可靠性的联合机制。

Method: 提出Think Short, Defer Smart (TSDS)框架：(1) 轻量级收敛探针，在动作稳定时提前停止on-device推理；(2) 基于困惑度的延迟规则，将不确定动作交给云端模型；(3) 通过多目标Learn-Then-Test (LTT)过程联合校准两个机制，提供有限样本保证。

Result: 在GSM8K、HotpotQA、MBPP和家政机器人四个基准上，TSDS相比仅延迟基线在HotpotQA、MBPP和机器人任务上减少43%-73%的每episode思考计算量，同时保持有保证的奖励和云端调用率。

Conclusion: TSDS通过联合优化早停和延迟机制，有效平衡边缘agent的推理效率与可靠性，提供了理论保证下的计算成本大幅降低。

Abstract: LLM agents following the ReAct paradigm are promising enablers of complex multi-step tasks, including multi-hop question answering, code generation, and control of physical AI systems. Yet, when deployed at the edge, they must tightly manage their reasoning budget while remaining reliable and deferring to a cloud-side model only when local uncertainty is too high to act safely. We propose Think Short, Defer Smart (TSDS), a framework that synergistically integrates a lightweight convergence probe, which halts on-device reasoning once the intended action has stabilized, with a perplexity-based deferral rule that escalates uncertain actions to a cloud-side model. Both mechanisms are jointly calibrated on end-to-end episode trajectories via a multi-objective Learn-Then-Test (LTT) procedure, providing simultaneous finite-sample guarantees on expected episode reward and cloud-call rate. We evaluate TSDS on four ReAct benchmarks spanning arithmetic reasoning (GSM8K), multi-hop question answering (HotpotQA), code generation (MBPP), and multi-step embodied planning (household robot), and compare against thought-calibration-only and calibrated-deferral-only standalone baselines. TSDS reduces per-episode thinking compute by 43%-73% over deferral-only baselines across HotpotQA, MBPP, and the household robot task, while maintaining certified reward and cloud-call rate guarantees.

</details>


### [92] [Conformalized Rate-Adaptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26887)
*Jiawei Yang,Yao Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CoRAS是一种自适应选择采集或压缩率的方法，能在高概率下将重建误差控制在目标水平以下，通过早期重建行为校准停止时间，在图像数据集上实现了目标覆盖率，平均使用更少测量，并为难以重建的图像分配更多测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率成像系统面临一个基本问题：何时采集的测量数据足以准确重建图像？现有固定速率方法无法适应不同图像的复杂度差异，需要一种自适应停止规则。

Method: 提出Conformalized Rate-Adaptive Sensing (CoRAS)方法，利用早期决策时间前的重建路径估计目标停止时间（重建误差首次低于目标水平的时间），然后使用具有相似早期重建行为的图像对该估计进行校准，生成具有边际和近似条件覆盖保证的停止时间上界。

Result: 在图像数据集上，CoRAS达到了目标停止时间覆盖率，平均使用的测量数少于固定速率停止规则，并为更难重建的图像分配更多测量。

Conclusion: CoRAS提供了一种有效的自适应采集速率控制方法，能在保证重建精度的同时优化测量效率，特别适用于不同图像复杂度差异较大的场景。

Abstract: Many high-resolution imaging systems face the same fundamental question: when have enough measurements been collected to reconstruct an image accurately? We develop Conformalized Rate-Adaptive Sensing (CoRAS), a method that adaptively chooses an acquisition or compression rate for each image while keeping the reconstruction error below a target level with high probability. As measurements are collected, an image reconstruction model gradually recovers the true image, producing a reconstruction path over acquisition rates. CoRAS uses this path up to an early decision time to estimate the target stopping time, defined as the first time at which the reconstruction error falls below the target level. It then calibrates this estimate using images with similar early reconstruction behavior, producing an upper bound on the stopping time with marginal and approximate conditional coverage guarantees. Experiments on image datasets show that CoRAS attains the target stopping-time coverage, uses fewer measurements on average than fixed-rate stopping rules, and assigns more measurements to images that are harder to reconstruct.

</details>


### [93] [Breaking the Curse with BAND: Nonparametric Distribution Estimation in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26955)
*Shuo-Chieh Huang,Chien-Ming Chi,Jau-er Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出BAND方法，通过稀疏贝叶斯网络结合条件均值估计，在高维混合类型时间序列数据中实现多项式总变差收敛率，有效缓解了维度灾难问题，经验性能优于多种基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多元分布估计的最优极小化收敛速度受维度灾难影响，在高维场景下效率极低。本文旨在通过稀疏贝叶斯网络结构突破这一瓶颈，实现更快的收敛率。

Method: 采用稀疏贝叶斯网络建模联合分布，每个条件概率通过稀疏感知的条件均值方法估计。该框架能处理混合数据类型的高维时间序列，并允许特征维度随样本量多项式增长。

Result: 实现了多项式总变差收敛率，显著快于缺乏稀疏性的经典多元直方图密度估计器的最优速率。经验评估表明，在数据采样和置信区间预测任务中，BAND与多种最新基准方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 稀疏贝叶斯网络方法能有效缓解高维分布估计中的维度灾难问题，在理论上取得更优收敛率，在实践中表现良好。

Abstract: Minimax-optimal rates for multivariate distribution estimation are known to suffer from the curse of dimensionality. We propose a sparse Bayesian network approach in which each conditional probability is estimated using sparsity-aware conditional mean methods. The resulting estimator, \textit{BAyesian Network Distribution regression} (BAND), handles mixed data types in high-dimensional time series and achieves polynomial total variation convergence rates while allowing the feature dimension to grow polynomially with the sample size. These rates are substantially faster than the classical optimal rates for multivariate histogram density estimators that lack sparsity. Empirical evaluations show that BAND performs competitively for data sampling and confidence region forecasting against a range of state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [Feature Bagging Provides Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26964)
*Yuheng Ma,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从算法稳定性角度研究特征袋装（feature bagging），引入特征不稳定性（FI）概念，证明特征袋装能提升稳定性，且适度轮数即可接近无限袋装稳定性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从实例角度分析袋装方法的稳定性（实例不稳定性II），但缺乏从特征维度理解稳定性与泛化能力的关系。本文旨在填补这一空白，从特征轴角度研究特征袋装的稳定性优势。

Method: 引入特征不稳定性（FI）作为特征轴上的稳定性度量；在参数线性模型和无模型（递归特征子采样，如随机森林）两种设定下，从理论上证明特征袋装能提升FI稳定性，并推导接近无限袋装稳定性所需的轮数。

Result: 实验表明FI能捕获与II互补的泛化相关信息；理论证明特征袋装相对于非袋装方法显著提升稳定性，且子采样越激进提升越大；适度数量的袋装轮数即可接近无限袋装稳定水平。

Conclusion: 特征袋装通过降低特征不稳定性来增强泛化能力，为理解随机森林等基于特征子采样的集成方法提供了新的稳定性视角。

Abstract: We study feature bagging through the lens of algorithmic stability. Feature bagging is an ensemble strategy that aggregates base learners trained on randomly subsampled feature subsets, possibly in a data-dependent manner. We introduce feature instability (FI), the feature-axis analogue of instance instability (II), which measures sensitivity to removing a single feature. Smaller values of II or FI correspond to stronger stability, and our experiments show that FI captures generalization-relevant information complementary to II. Within this framework, we analyze feature bagging in both a parametric linear model and a model-free setting inspired by recursive feature subsampling in random forests. In both settings, we establish formal guarantees showing that feature bagging improves the relevant stability relative to its non-bagged counterpart, with larger improvements under more aggressive subsampling. We further show that a modest number of bagging rounds is sufficient to approach the infinite-bagging stability level.

</details>


### [95] [PIKS: Universal Physics-Informed Kernel Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27062)
*Joachim Bona-Pellissier,Giacomo Meanti,Matteo Santacesaria,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了物理信息核方法（PIKS），证明了其对线性微分约束的普适一致性，推导了有限样本误差界，并在数值实验中展示了与PINNs和有限元方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）虽在应用中占主导地位，但其复杂架构和优化景观阻碍了相应学习理论的发展；而现有核方法仅能处理目标属于原生RKHS的良设定情形，这对物理目标施加了不现实的规则性假设。因此需要一种兼具理论可解释性与实际有效性的物理信息学习方法。

Method: 将经典核方法的算子理论分析扩展到物理信息机器学习，提出物理信息核方法（PIKS），利用通用核（如高斯核、Matérn核）处理线性微分约束，并建立普适一致性与有限样本误差界。

Result: 证明了PIKS对于线性微分约束具有普适一致性，即使用通用核时估计器能够渐进学习目标并满足物理约束；推导了源条件假设下的有限样本误差界；数值实验表明PIKS可与PINNs和传统有限元方法竞争。

Conclusion: PIKS提供了物理信息学习的一个理论严谨且实用的替代方案，弥补了核方法在物理信息机器学习中的理论空白，有望在实际应用中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning incorporates physical principles --often expressed via differential operators-- into data-driven models. While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) dominate empirical applications, the complexity of neural network architectures and optimization landscapes hinders the development of a corresponding learning theory. In turn, kernel methods offer an appealing alternative with closed-form solutions and analytical tractability, yet existing guarantees primarily cover the well-specified setting where the target belongs to the native Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS). This imposes unrealistic regularity assumptions that physical targets often fail to satisfy. In this paper, we introduce and analyze Physics-Informed Kernel methodS (PIKS). We establish the universal consistency of PIKS for linear differential constraints, proving that for universal kernels (such as Gaussian or Matérn), the estimator asymptotically learns the target while satisfying physical constraints. We further derive finite-sample bounds under suitable source conditions. Our analysis is based on extending classical operator-theoretic analysis of kernel methods to physics-informed machine learning. Numerical experiments demonstrate that PIKS can be competitive with PINNs and traditional finite element methods.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Inertial Primal Dual Dynamics with Hessian-driven Damping for Smooth and Bilinearly Coupled Saddle Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26235)
*Zepeng Wang,Juan Peypouquet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种带有Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性原始-对偶动力系统，用于求解具有双线性耦合的光滑鞍点问题。对于凸-凹函数，原始-对偶间隙收敛率为$\mathcal{O}(1/t^2)$；对于强凸-强凹函数，在未知强凸参数时渐近率为$\mathcal{O}(1/t^{α-1})$（$α\ge3$），已知时则加速线性收敛。此外，还将其应用于仿射约束凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为求解具有双线性耦合的光滑鞍点问题设计更高效的动力学系统，并利用Hessian驱动阻尼提升收敛性能。

Method: 提出两种带有Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性原始-对偶动力系统，分别针对凸-凹和强凸-强凹情形进行分析，并扩展到仿射约束凸优化问题。

Result: 凸-凹情形：原始-对偶间隙收敛率$\mathcal{O}(1/t^2)$；强凸-强凹情形：未知强凸参数时渐近率$\mathcal{O}(1/t^{α-1})$ ($α\ge3$)，已知时加速线性收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的惯性系统有效提升了光滑鞍点问题的求解效率，并为仿射约束凸优化提供了新的动力学方法。

Abstract: Featuring Hessian-driven damping, two inertial primal dual dynamical systems are proposed for solving smooth saddle point problems with bilinear coupling. For convex-concave functions, we establish a convergence rate $\mathcal{O}\left( \frac{1}{t^2} \right)$ for the primal dual gap; for strongly convex-strongly concave functions, we obtain an asymptotic rate $\mathcal{O}\left( \frac{1}{t^{α-1}} \right)$ ($α\ge 3$ is the damping parameter) without knowledge of the strong convexity parameters, and an accelerated linear convergence rate when the strong convexity parameters are known. As an application of the proposed inertial systems, we also consider the affinely constrained convex optimization problem, and develop an inertial system with Hessian-driven damping, which complements existing results.

</details>


### [97] [Symmetrized Sinkhorn-Gibbs Inference for Oscillatory Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26264)
*Gabriel Huerta,Mohammad Motamed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对振荡逆问题中信号错位和周期跳跃导致的非凸损失景观，本文提出了一种对称化Sinkhorn-Gibbs推断框架。通过对有符号信号进行归一化处理并结合对称化Sinkhorn损失，将其纳入Gibbs后验框架，实现了对振荡数据的稳健推断。理论证明了损失的光滑性、Gibbs后验的良定义性和鲁棒性，数值实验表明该方法在经验风险景观、后验推断精度、噪声鲁棒性和群体恢复方面优于基于欧几里得和迹Wasserstein损失的Gibbs后验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡逆问题中由于信号错位和周期跳跃现象，其差异景观高度非凸，给不确定性量化带来巨大挑战。虽然Gibbs后验提供了灵活框架，但其性能强烈依赖于所选差异度量下的经验风险景观。经典最优传输虽能考虑振荡信号的空间和时间结构，但无法直接应用于有符号信号（不满足正性要求）。因此需要一种能够处理有符号振荡信号的新型推断方法。

Method: 提出对称化Sinkhorn-Gibbs推断框架：(1) 对有符号信号进行归一化处理；(2) 设计对称化Sinkhorn损失，利用归一化信号及其归一化否定的互补传输信息；(3) 将该损失纳入Gibbs后验框架；(4) 建立损失的光滑性、后验良定义性和鲁棒性理论；(5) 开发自适应采样策略进行后验计算。

Result: 数值实验表明：(1) 经验风险景观振荡更少，虚假局部极小值更少；(2) 后验推断更准确；(3) 对观测噪声具有更强的鲁棒性；(4) 群体级恢复效果优于基于欧几里得损失和迹Wasserstein损失的Gibbs后验方法。

Conclusion: 对称化Sinkhorn-Gibbs推断框架有效解决了振荡逆问题中非凸差异景观带来的挑战，为有符号振荡信号的不确定性量化提供了一种理论完备且实践有效的推断方法。

Abstract: Oscillatory inverse problems often exhibit highly nonconvex discrepancy landscapes due to signal misalignment and cycle-skipping phenomena, posing significant challenges for uncertainty quantification. While Gibbs posteriors provide a flexible framework for incorporating problem-specific discrepancy measures, their performance depends strongly on the empirical risk landscape induced by the chosen discrepancy measure. Optimal transport accounts for the spatial and temporal structure of the underlying feature domain when comparing oscillatory signals. However, the direct application of classical optimal transport is precluded because observed and predicted signals are typically signed and therefore do not satisfy the positivity requirements of classical transport formulations. We introduce a symmetrized Sinkhorn-Gibbs inference framework for oscillatory inverse problems. The proposed approach combines a normalization procedure for signed signals with a symmetrized Sinkhorn loss that exploits complementary transport information from the normalized signals and their normalized negations. The resulting loss is incorporated into the Gibbs posterior framework, yielding a Gibbs inference methodology tailored to oscillatory data. We establish smoothness properties of the proposed loss, prove well-definedness of the resulting Gibbs posterior, derive robustness guarantees, and develop an adaptive sampling strategy for posterior computation. Numerical experiments demonstrate less oscillatory empirical risk landscapes with fewer spurious local minima, more accurate posterior inference, greater robustness to observational noise, and improved population-level recovery than Gibbs posteriors based on Euclidean and trace-wise Wasserstein losses.

</details>


### [98] [Scalable Dynamic Optimal Transport via Distributed Linearized ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26407)
*Hari Dahal,Rongjie Lai,Yangyang Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对动态最优传输问题中的两个核心挑战——近零密度区域的数值不稳定性和大规模时空离散化带来的内存负担，提出了一种结合精确近端映射的LADMM算法与分布式计算框架的解决方案，实验验证了其稳定性、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态最优传输问题在数值求解时面临两大挑战：(1) 初始/终端密度趋近于零时，目标函数Lipschitz常数与密度立方成反比，导致传统方法收敛缓慢甚至发散；(2) 精细时空网格离散化需要存储全时空域变量，内存开销随网格细化和维度增加而急剧增长。

Method: (1) 对经典离散化动态OT问题进行重构，使目标函数具有精确近端映射；(2) 结合线性化交替方向乘子法(LADMM)，在密度可能为零的困难场景下仍保持稳定；(3) 引入分布式公式，将优化变量划分到多个智能体，降低单个智能体存储需求并实现计算加速。

Result: 在一维和二维空间设置下进行了不同难度级别的数值实验，结果表明所提方法相比传统方法在稳定性、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了动态OT中近零密度数值不稳定和大规模存储两个关键瓶颈，为高维精细网格下的动态OT求解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address two fundamental challenges in the numerical solution of dynamic opti- mal transport (OT) problems. The first challenge arises when the initial and/or terminal densities approach zero and no positive lower bound is available. In this regime, conventional methods may become unstable or computationally inefficient, since the Lipschitz constant of the objective can scale like the reciprocal of the cube of the density. As a result, near-zero regions may lead to slow convergence or even divergence. The second challenge concerns the substantial memory cost of the dynamic formulation, whose discretization over fine spatial and temporal grids requires storing vari- ables across the entire space-time domain. This storage burden quickly becomes prohibitive as the grid is refined or the spatial dimension increases. To overcome the first difficulty, we reformulate the classical discretized dynamic OT problem so that the resulting objective admits an exact proximal mapping. When combined with a linearized alternating direction method of multipliers (LADMM), this reformulation yields an efficient and robust algorithm that remains stable even in challenging settings where the density may vanish. To reduce the memory burden, we further introduce a dis- tributed formulation in which the optimization variables are partitioned across multiple agents. This design substantially lowers the storage requirement for each agent and can also lead to computational acceleration. We validate the proposed framework through numerical experiments in one- and two- dimensional spatial settings under varying levels of difficulty. The results demonstrate the stability, robustness, and scalability of the proposed method in comparison with conventional approaches.

</details>


### [99] [Model-Free Q-Learning for Infinite-Horizon Stochastic Linear Quadratic Problems with Regime Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26456)
*Xinyue Zhang,Na Li,Xun Li,Zuo Quan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无限时域连续时间随机线性二次型最优控制问题（含马尔可夫切换），提出了一种基于自适应动态规划的无模型控制方法，包括on-policy和off-policy Q学习算法，并通过理论证明和数值实验验证了算法的稳定性、收敛性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型驱动方法在处理含体制切换的连续时间随机LQ最优控制问题时依赖于精确的系统模型，而实际系统中模型难以获取或存在不确定性，因此需要向无模型的自适应动态规划方法进行范式转换。

Method: 采用自适应动态规划框架，分别设计了on-policy和off-policy Q学习算法，利用在线状态轨迹数据学习最优控制器；理论证明了两种策略架构的等价性，并进行了闭环系统稳定性与算法收敛性的严格分析；数值实现中采用向量化和Kronecker积代数提高计算效率。

Result: 理论证明了on-policy与off-policy架构的等价性，验证了闭环系统的稳定性以及算法收敛到最优解；数值算例表明所提出的无模型控制策略在实际操作中有效且可行。

Conclusion: 提出的无模型自适应动态规划方法成功解决了含体制切换的连续时间随机LQ最优控制问题，为复杂随机系统的在线控制提供了可行的理论框架和计算方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses infinite-horizon continuous-time stochastic linear quadratic optimal control problems with regime switching. We propose a paradigm shift from model-based design by adopting an adaptive dynamic programming approach, specifically developing on-policy and off-policy Q-learning algorithms that learn the optimal controller solely from online state trajectory data. The theoretical core of our work consists of a complete proof of the equivalence between the on- and off-policy architectures, alongside a rigorous analysis establishing the stability of the closed-loop system and the convergence of the algorithms to the optimal solution. For computational tractability, we implement these algorithms using vectorization and Kronecker product algebra. The theoretical results are corroborated by numerical case studies that clearly demonstrate the operational effectiveness and practical feasibility of the proposed model-free control strategy.

</details>


### [100] [A Receding Horizon Control For General Assembly Line Balancing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26482)
*Ali Mohamed Ali,Luca Tirel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于滚动时域最优控制框架的通用装配线平衡问题（GALBP）新方法，通过离散化建模和混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）求解，实现了任务分配与资源调度的协同优化，有效最小化完工时间并适应动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统装配线平衡方法通常依赖于特定的线体配置假设，缺乏灵活性和对动态变化的适应能力。本文旨在提出一种更通用、鲁棒的建模与优化框架，以更灵活地处理不同配置的装配线平衡问题。

Method: 采用滚动时域最优控制框架，构建了装配线的离散模型（避免特定线体配置假设）；控制动作为任务分配矩阵和资源分配矩阵，分别将任务指派到工作站、将资源分配到工作站；优化问题建模为混合整数非线性规划（MINLP），利用滚动时域的鲁棒性实现动态适应。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的控制综合方法能够高效地分配任务和资源，最小化整体完工时间，并展现出良好的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 滚动时域最优控制框架为解决通用装配线平衡问题提供了一种灵活且鲁棒的解决方案，能够有效适应突发变化，在任务与资源的协同分配中表现出色。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel approach to the General Assembly Line Balancing Problem (GALBP) by utilizing a receding horizon optimal control framework. The proposed discrete model for the assembly line offers a flexible representation, avoiding assumptions about specific line configurations. The control actions aim to optimize the industrial assembly line by minimizing the completion time while adhering to constraints such as task precedence, workstation capacity, and resource requirements. Control actions are represented through task assignment and resource allocation matrices, assigning tasks to specific workstations and assigning resources to workstations, respectively. The optimization problem is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programming (MINLP) problem. The inherent robustness of the receding horizon approach ensures optimal solutions for the assembly line, effectively adapting to sudden changes. Numerical experiments demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the proposed control synthesis in efficiently distributing tasks and resources, minimizing the overall completion time.

</details>


### [101] [A new theorem of alternatives leading to sufficient conditions for the superiorization guarantee question of Dynamic String-Averaging in the inconsistent case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26489)
*Kay Barshad,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非一致情形（输入算子无公共不动点）下，将超化方法论（SM）应用于通用动态弦平均（GDSA）方法，旨在实现凸可行性同时降低目标函数值。提出了一个新的"备选定理"，并给出了保证超化版本收敛到"更优"可行点的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多科学与实际问题中，精确求解约束最优化问题在时间、能量和资源上成本高昂。超化方法论提供了一种实用且高效的替代方案，但现有文献对其能否真正产生"更优"结果的理论保证仅做了部分研究。

Method: 在非一致情形下，将超化方法论（SM）与通用动态弦平均（GDSA）算法结合，提出一个新的"备选定理"（theorem of alternatives），并据此推导出保证超化版本收敛到更优可行点的充分条件。

Result: 提出了一个新的"备选定理"，给出了新的充分条件，保证超化后的GDSA算法收敛到目标函数值小于或等于未扰动可行性搜索算法所产生结果的可行点。

Conclusion: 在非一致情形下，超化方法论可以成功应用于GDSA算法，且在一定的充分条件下，能够保证得到"更优"的可行解，为高效求解约束优化问题提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study the Superiorization Methodology (SM) in the context of the General Dynamic String-Averaging (GDSA) method in the inconsistent case (that is, where the input operators don't have a common fixed point) which primarily aims at achieving convex feasibility while simultaneously reducing an objective function. In many scientific and real-world problems modeled as constrained minimization tasks, striving for the exact constrained optimum can be costly in terms of time, energy, and resources. Therefore, applying the SM can offer a practical and efficient alternative. In particular, we present a new "theorem of alternatives" for the superiorization method which leads to investigation of theoretical conditions under which the superiorized version of the GDSA algorithm converges to a "superior" feasible point, i.e., one with an objective function value that is smaller or equal to that produced by the unperturbed feasibility-seeking algorithm. While this question has only been partially addressed in the existing literature, we present new sufficient conditions that guarantee that the SM attains such a superior outcome.

</details>


### [102] [A Primal Approach to Facial Reduction for SDP Relaxations of Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26514)
*Hao Hu,Mingming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向组合优化问题SDP松弛的面部约简算法，利用可行解结构将预处理速度平均提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准面部约简算法在处理组合优化问题的半定规划松弛时效率较低，未能充分利用问题特性和可用的可行解信息。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的面部约简算法，专门针对具有二次目标函数的组合优化问题的半定规划松弛。该方法利用这些松弛的特定结构，特别是实际中常能高效生成可行解的特点，将可行解融入面部约简过程以简化约简步骤。

Result: 在考虑的基准测试集上，该面部约简算法比标准实现平均快4倍，显著改进了组合优化中SDP松弛的预处理性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用可行解信息，所提出的面部约简算法能有效加速组合优化问题SDP松弛的预处理过程。

Abstract: We propose a novel facial reduction algorithm tailored to semidefinite programming relaxations of combinatorial optimization problems with quadratic objective functions. Our method leverages the specific structure of these relaxations, particularly the availability of feasible solutions that can often be generated efficiently in practice. By incorporating such solutions into the facial reduction process, we substantially simplify the reduction steps. On average, our facial reduction algorithm is four times faster than the standard implementation on the considered benchmark sets, providing significantly improved preprocessing for SDP relaxations in combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Gradient-Based Methods for a Broader Class of Optimization Problems under Performative Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26562)
*Hiroki Hamaguchi,Yuya Hikima,Hiroshi Sawada,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度的优化方法，用于在表现性预测（模型部署影响未来数据分布）下进行参数优化。该方法通过有限差分显式估计分布偏移，在更弱的假设下提供收敛保证，适用于更广泛的损失函数和数据分布，并提出了减少样本量的实用变体。实验表明算法收敛更快更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在表现性预测下的梯度方法通常需要对数据分布或损失函数有较强的假设限制，导致实际应用受限。本文旨在克服这些局限性，提出在更弱假设下具有收敛保证的优化方法。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的优化方法，通过有限差分显式估计模型部署导致的分布偏移，从而计算修正后的梯度更新方向。同时提出一种实用变体，通过减少所需样本数量提高效率。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提算法相比现有方法收敛速度更快且收敛结果更稳定。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在更宽松的假设条件下实现了收敛保证，扩展了表现性预测优化的适用范围，具有更好的实际可用性。

Abstract: We study optimization under performative prediction, where deploying a model affects the future data distribution. For this setting, several gradient-based approaches have been proposed. However, they typically assume specific data distributions or loss functions, which limit their practical applicability. To overcome these limitations, we propose a gradient-based optimization method with convergence guarantees under substantially weaker assumptions. Our method explicitly estimates the induced distribution shift through finite differences. It enables higher-dimensional optimization across broader classes of loss functions and data distributions. We also propose a practical variant that reduces the number of samples required. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our proposed algorithms converge faster and more consistently than existing ones.

</details>


### [104] [Optimal control problems for quasi-linear parabolic equations and their linear approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26585)
*Xu Liu,Meisai Wang,Xu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明当初始数据、目标和控制作用足够小时，受控拟线性抛物型方程的最优控制问题可以用其线性近似精确逼近，并给出了内点与边界点的验证准则，证明了拟线性与线性问题的最优控制及轨线之差是初始数据的小高阶无穷小量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受热扩散模型启发，当初始数据、目标和控制作用足够小时，可将受控拟线性抛物型方程形式化地替换为其线性近似，但需从最优控制问题层面严格证明这一近似的精确性。

Method: 首先建立验证准则区分最优控制是容许控制集的内点还是边界点；然后证明拟线性与线性抛物型方程的最优控制及对应最优轨线的差异相对于成本泛函中的小初始数据和小目标是高阶无穷小量。

Result: 对于小数据，拟线性方程控制问题可由对应线性问题精确近似；在内点情形下逼近阶是锐利的（sharp）。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上严格证明了小数据条件下拟线性最优控制问题的线性化近似的合理性，并给出了逼近阶的精确保证。

Abstract: Motivated by thermal diffusion models, a controlled quasi-linear parabolic equation can be formally replaced by its linear approximation, when the initial datum, target and control actions are sufficiently small. This paper makes this approximation precise at the level of optimal control problems. First, we provide verification criteria which distinguish whether an optimal control is an interior point or a boundary point of the admissible control set. We then prove that the differences between the optimal controls and the corresponding optimal trajectories for the quasi-linear and linear parabolic equations are higher-order infinitesimals with respect to the small initial datum and target appearing in the cost functional. Consequently, for small data, the optimal control problem governed by the quasi-linear equation can be approximated by the corresponding linear problem. In the interior-point case, the resulting approximation order is sharp.

</details>


### [105] [Mesh-dependent iteration count growth in primal-dual active set strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26622)
*Ioannis P. A. Papadopoulos,Michael Hintermüller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了原始-对偶积极集策略（PDAS）在求解障碍问题、Signorini问题等变分不等式时，随着网格细化出现的迭代次数增长问题：Signorini问题呈线性增长，障碍问题呈指数增长（每细化一次迭代翻倍）。作者通过理论分析解释了这一现象，指出障碍问题中节点自由度只能逐层脱离接触集，并推导了依赖于对偶可行性违反程度的全局收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDAS（等价于半光滑牛顿法SSN）是求解带逐点不等式约束的混合互补问题的流行迭代求解器，在离散无限维问题时理论上具有局部超线性收敛性。然而实践中发现，对于许多问题类别，随着网格细化，达到收敛所需的迭代次数会无界增长，这与理论预期相悖。本文旨在数值研究并理论解释这一反常现象。

Method: 数值实验：在均匀细化网格上系统测试PDAS对障碍问题、Signorini问题及相关模型的迭代次数变化。理论分析：(i) 证明障碍问题中节点自由度在去激活阶段只能逐层脱离障碍层；(ii) 推导依赖于对偶可行性违反程度的一般全局收敛率；(iii) 证明在无限维设定下，该收敛率导致某些问题失去局部超线性收敛，另一些问题甚至发散。

Result: Signorini问题：网格细化时每次细化增加少量迭代，呈线性增长。障碍问题：PDAS停滞，迭代次数呈指数增长（每细化一次近似翻倍）。理论结果解释了为何无限维设定下PDAS对某些问题仍能良好定义但失去局部超线性收敛，对另一些问题则发散。

Conclusion: PDAS在无限维问题离散化后，网格细化会导致迭代次数增长甚至发散，这源于对偶可行性违反程度与网格尺寸的耦合关系。障碍问题的逐层脱离机制是迭代指数增长的根本原因。

Abstract: Primal-dual active set strategies (PDAS) are popular iterative solvers for mixed complementarity problems such as constrained optimization problems with pointwise inequality constraints. Examples include the reduced-space active set algorithm vinewtonrsls found in PETSc. When applied to discretized infinite-dimensional problems, PDAS exhibit local superlinear convergence thanks to their equivalence to a semismooth Newton method (SSN). However, for many problem classes the number of iterations, to reach convergence, grows without bound under mesh refinement. In this paper we numerically study PDAS iteration counts on uniformly refined meshes for obstacle problems, Signorini problems, and related models. As the mesh size tends to zero, PDAS applied to Signorini-type problems lose their local superlinear convergence, resulting in linear growth of the iteration count (adding some iterations with each refinement). For obstacle problems, PDAS stagnates, leading to exponential iteration growth (asymptotically doubling with each refinement). We explain these phenomena by (i) proving that, for obstacle problems, nodal degrees of freedom only peel away from the obstacle layer-by-layer during the deactivation phase, (ii) deriving a general global convergence rate for PDAS that depends on the magnitude of dual feasibility violation, and (iii) demonstrating why, in the infinite-dimensional setting, this leads to a well-defined solver, but without local superlinear convergence, for some problems yet divergence for others.

</details>


### [106] [Equilibrium for Regular-Singular Control under Mean-Variance Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26635)
*Zongxia Liang,Xiaodong Luo,Jiayu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究均值-方差准则下的混合正则-奇异控制问题，在人际博弈框架中寻求时间一致均衡策略，提出新均衡概念并给出验证定理与必要条件。以再保险问题为例构建显式耦合均衡解，展示正则与奇异控制的复杂耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合正则-奇异控制在均值-方差准则下的时间一致均衡策略缺乏系统的数学刻画，需要建立新的均衡概念和理论框架来完整描述此类问题。

Method: 采用人际博弈设定，提出新型均衡概念；通过验证定理和必要条件给出均衡的完整数学刻画；利用再保险问题构建显式耦合均衡解进行理论验证。

Result: 成功推导出验证定理和必要条件；在再保险问题中构建了显式耦合均衡解，其中正则控制依赖于奇异控制状态，奇异控制的自由边界随正则控制表达式变化而动态切换；退化情形下耦合解退化为两个独立单控制均衡的组合。

Conclusion: 所提出的均衡概念和理论框架能够有效刻画混合正则-奇异控制问题的时间一致均衡策略，耦合结构在退化情形下与独立单控制均衡一致，保证了理论的一致性。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of mixed regular-singular control problems under mean-variance criteria. We seek time-consistent equilibrium strategies in an intrapersonal game setting and propose a novel equilibrium notion. Under which, we derive a verification theorem and necessary conditions providing a full mathematical characterization of the equilibrium. To illustrate the theory, we construct explicit coupled equilibrium solutions for a reinsurance problem, where the regular control depends on the singular control state, and the free boundary of the singular control switches dynamically in accordance with the variation of the regular control expression, yielding nontrivial coupling. In the degenerate case $α_2=0$, the coupled solution reduces to the combination of two independent single-control equilibria and coincides with the limit as the parameter tends to zero.

</details>


### [107] [On Mean-field Singular Stochastic Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26808)
*Andrea Amato,Federico Cannerozzi,Giorgio Ferrari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域下具有奇异控制的一类平均场控制（MFC）问题，推导出相应的势平均场博弈（MFG），并在凸性假设下证明该势MFG的解即原MFC问题的解。将该结果应用于经典的Monotone Follower问题（带标量平均场交互），通过最优停止和Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理求解势MFG。在策略互补情形下，均衡由连续非增的自由边界刻画，该边界唯一满足一个非线性积分方程。这是首个在有限时域平均场奇异随机控制问题中完整刻画最优策略的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的平均场控制（MFC）研究多处理正则控制问题，而现实中的许多经济、金融和工程问题涉及不可逆决策（如投资、产能调整），其最优策略为奇异控制形式。如何在平均场框架下有效求解这类问题缺乏系统方法。本文旨在建立从MFC到势MFG的转化理论，并利用该理论求解带平均场交互的经典Monotone Follower问题。

Method: 1. 建立一般MFC问题与势MFG之间的联系，在凸性假设下证明势MFG解即原MFC解；2. 将理论应用于带标量平均场交互的Monotone Follower问题，将优化步骤转化为最优停止问题；3. 利用Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg不动点定理证明势MFG均衡的存在性；4. 在策略互补情形下，通过非线性积分方程表征自由边界。

Result: 在策略互补（strategic complementarities）条件下，平均场均衡由一条连续非增的自由边界唯一确定，该边界满足一个非线性积分方程。这是有限时域平均场奇异随机控制问题中首次对最优策略的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 本文为带奇异控制的平均场控制问题提供了一套完整的分析框架，通过转化为势平均场博弈实现了问题的求解，并首次在有限时域下给出了平均场奇异控制问题最优策略的完整刻画。该工作填补了平均场控制理论在不可逆决策场景下的空白

Abstract: We study a class of mean-field control (MFC) problems with singular controls over a finite horizon, allowing for general dependence of the cost functional on the measure argument. We derive an auxiliary mean-field game (MFG) with singular controls, which we refer to as a potential MFG, and show that, under suitable convexity assumptions, any solution to this potential MFG yields a solution to the original MFC problem. We apply this general result to a version of the classical Monotone Follower Problem by I. Karatzas and S. E. Shreve (SIAM Journal on Control and Optimization 22(6), pp. 856-877, 1984) with scalar mean-field interaction. The associated potential MFG with singular controls is solved by exploiting its connection with optimal stopping for the optimization step and by a suitable application of the Kakutani-Fan-Glicksberg fixed-point theorem. In the case of strategic complementarities, the mean-field equilibrium (and hence the optimal policy of the original MFC problem) is characterized by a continuous nonincreasing free boundary that uniquely solves a nonlinear integral equation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper to provide a complete characterization of the optimal policy in a finite-horizon mean-field singular stochastic control problem.

</details>


### [108] [Minimax adaptive control for finite sets of positive linear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26816)
*Fethi Bencherki,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有参数不确定性和对抗性扰动的离散时间正线性系统，提出了一种极小极大自适应控制框架，通过将问题重新表述为标准的极小极大两人零和动态博弈，利用极小极大动态规划求解Bellman不等式，得到了无需初始稳定控制器即可保持稳定性和正性的策略，并具有ℓ1增益鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正线性系统的自适应控制方法在处理参数不确定性和对抗性扰动时缺乏鲁棒性保证，且通常需要初始稳定控制器，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将问题建模为控制器与对手之间的动态博弈，通过等价重构转化为标准极小极大两人零和动态博弈，利用极小极大动态规划求解Bellman不等式，推导出保持稳定性和正性的显式策略。

Result: 得到的控制器具有有界ℓ1增益鲁棒性保证，在参数充分估计后退化为标准H∞型最优控制器，数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为不确定正线性系统提供了兼具自适应性和鲁棒性的控制方案，无需初始稳定控制器且保证系统正性，理论结果与实验一致。

Abstract: We present a minimax adaptive control framework for discrete-time positive linear systems with parametric uncertainty and adversarial disturbances. The uncertainty in the system dynamics is assumed to lie in a finite set of possible plants. We formulate the problem as a dynamic game between the controller, which minimizes the cost, and an adversary, which selects both the disturbances and the plant dynamics to maximize the cost. An equivalent reformulation of the original game transforms the problem into a standard minimax two-player zero-sum dynamic game. This enables the problem to be addressed via minimax dynamic programming. We provide an explicit solution to the Bellman inequality, yielding stabilizing, positivity preserving policies without requiring an initially stabilizing controller. The resulting controller enjoys robustness guarantees in the form of bounded $\ell_1$-gain from disturbances to errors. Once the uncertain parameters have been sufficiently estimated, the controller behaves like a standard $\mathcal H_\infty$-type optimal controller for positive linear systems. The theoretical findings are supported by numerical experiments illustrating the resulting adaptive controller in action.

</details>


### [109] [Stabilizability of neural fields from thick subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26883)
*Clemens Bombach,Marco Ragni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经场稳定化问题中执行器受限于固定子集的情况，证明了在适当相互作用强度和相对密度条件下，线性化神经场可实现开环稳定，并推导出传感器/执行器约束下的闭环稳定化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经工程中神经场的稳定化是一个重要问题，但以往研究常假设执行器可任意放置，这在实际应用中难以实现。作者希望探究当控制输入只能作用于神经场中一个固定子集时，系统是否仍能稳定。

Method: 对Amari型神经场进行线性化分析，假设控制集满足立方体与控制集的交集体积存在下界的几何条件，在合适的相互作用强度和相对密度假设下证明开环稳定化，进而推广到闭环稳定化，并用数值仿真验证。

Result: 证明了线性化神经场在控制集满足相对密度条件时是开环可稳定的，并由此推导出传感器/执行器约束下的闭环稳定化结果，数值仿真提供了控制成本的估算。

Conclusion: 即使执行器只能固定在神经场的某个子集上，只要控制集满足一定的密度条件且相互作用强度适当，神经场的稳定化依然是可行的。

Abstract: An important problem in neuro-engineering is the stabilization of neural fields. In applications, it is often assumed that the actuator placement can be chosen arbitrarily. In this work, we investigate the stabilizability of controlled Amari-type neural fields where the control input is prescribed to act only on a fixed subset of the neural field. We show that the linearized neural field is open-loop stabilizable under suitable assumptions on the interaction strength and a mild relative density assumption on the control set. Our geometric assumption requires that the volume of each cube intersected with the control set must be bounded below. As a consequence, we derive closed-loop stabilizability of the neural fields, under sensor/actuator placement constraints. Numerical simulations are used to illustrate the results and obtain empirical estimates on the control cost.

</details>


### [110] [Normalized First-Order Methods for Convex (L0, L1)-Smooth Optimization with Inexact Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26969)
*Evgeniy Kovalev,Fedor Stonyakin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅能访问Comparison Oracle（返回带绝对误差的归一化梯度近似）的条件下，对凸(L0,L1)-光滑优化问题提出归一化梯度下降法和带Polyak步长的梯度下降法的变体，给出了保证收敛的近似误差上界和收敛速率，无需经典Lipschitz光滑假设或精确梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习和深度学习中的优化问题常违反经典梯度Lipschitz假设，需要广义光滑性（如(L0,L1)-光滑）建模；同时许多应用中精确梯度计算不切实际或计算昂贵，因此需要在不依赖精确梯度或经典光滑假设的前提下设计有效优化方法。

Method: 基于Comparison Oracle（以线性时间返回带界绝对误差的归一化梯度近似），提出其变体的归一化梯度下降法（NGD）和带Polyak步长的梯度下降法（GD-Polyak），推导保证收敛的近似误差上界并建立收敛速率。

Result: 建立了所提方法在凸(L0,L1)-光滑优化下的收敛率，给出了Comparison Oracle近似误差的可容许上界，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 在仅含Comparison Oracle而无经典光滑假设和精确梯度的条件下，所提方法仍能有效收敛，拓展了广义光滑优化的实际适用场景。

Abstract: Generalized smoothness, such as (L0, L1)-smoothness, have recently attracted considerable attention due to their ability to model optimization problems arising in modern machine and deep learning, where the classical Lipschitz assumptions of the gradient is often violated. At the same time, computing exact gradients may be impractical or computationally expensive in many applications. In this work, we study convex (L0, L1)-smooth optimization under access only to a normalized approximation recently proposed Comparison Oracle, which returns an inexact normalized gradient in linear time with a bounded absolute error. Within this framework, we develop comparison-oracle variants of Normalized Gradient Descent and Gradient Descent with Polyak stepsizes. We establish explicit upper bounds on the approximation error that guarantee convergence and derive convergence rates for all proposed methods. Unlike existing analyses, our results require neither classical smoothness assumptions nor access to exact gradients or their exact normalized counterparts. Finally, numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [111] [Dynamic output-feedback stabilization of uncertain linear dynamics via digital twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26995)
*Philipp A. Guth,Karl Kunisch,Sergio S. Rodrigues,Jesper Schröder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数字孪生框架，用于不确定动态系统的输出反馈镇定和参数辨识。虚拟模型与物理过程并行演化，实时同化测量数据，同时重构系统状态、生成镇定反馈，并通过贝叶斯方法从受控动力学数据中推断模型参数。数值结果展示了数字孪生作为观测器、参数估计器和控制代理的联合作用，确保不确定性下的鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不确定动态系统在输出反馈镇定和参数辨识方面的挑战，利用数字孪生技术实现状态重构、反馈控制和参数估计的统一框架。

Method: 提出数字孪生框架，包含虚拟模型与物理过程并行演化，实时同化测量数据；通过贝叶斯方法从受控动力学数据中推断模型参数；数字孪生同时重构系统状态并生成镇定反馈。

Result: 数值结果验证了数字孪生能够联合充当观测器、参数估计器和控制代理，在不确定性下保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 数字孪生框架可以有效整合状态观测、参数估计和反馈控制功能，为不确定动态系统提供鲁棒的性能保障。

Abstract: This work presents a digital twin framework for output-feedback stabilization and parameter identification in uncertain dynamical systems. A virtual model evolves in parallel with the physical process, assimilating measurement data in real time. By design, the digital twin reconstructs the system state and generates a stabilizing feedback, while model parameters are simultaneously inferred from data of the controlled dynamics using a Bayesian approach. Numerical results for the coupled physical-virtual dynamics demonstrate how digital twins can act jointly as observers, parameter estimators, and control agents, ensuring robust performance under uncertainty.

</details>


### [112] [Forcing and duality-corrected contracts for volatility control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27039)
*Alessandro Chiusolo,Emma Hubert,Dylan Possamaï,Nizar Touzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了连续时间委托-代理问题中的最优激励合同设计。针对Cvitanić等人(2018)提出的2BSDE方法依赖于一个可能不成立的假设(Assumption 2.3)的问题，本文引入了一类更一般的合同（由函数ψ参数化），并给出了两种自然规范：一种产生强制型合同，另一种修正了不满足该假设时的对偶间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优激励合同构建方法（Cvitanić等人, 2018）依赖于一个可能不普遍成立的假设（Assumption 2.3），需要更一般的合同框架来克服这一局限。

Method: 引入由参数函数ψ刻画的一般化合同类，满足使合同对代理人具有揭示性且对委托人无损失的条件；提出两种ψ的具体规范：一种基于BSDE方法导出强制型合同，另一种基于2BSDE方法修正对偶间隙。

Result: 构建了比现有方法更一般的合同框架，克服了Assumption 2.3不成立时的问题；给出了两种具有实际意义的ψ规范，分别对应强制型合同和对偶间隙修正合同。

Conclusion: 通过引入参数化的一般合同类，解决了连续时间委托-代理问题中现有最优性结果对特定假设的依赖问题，为合同设计提供了更灵活的框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the construction of optimal incentives in continuous-time principal-agent problems with drift and volatility control. Originally, a general approach relying on dynamic programming and second-order backward stochastic differential equations (2BSDEs) was developed by Cvitanić, Possamaï, and Touzi (2018) [8] to determine the optimal form of contracts in this setting. More recently, Chiusolo and Hubert (2026) [5] proposed a BSDE-based approach by introducing an alternative `contractible-volatility' problem for the principal. In addition to the proposed new method, this work highlights that the optimality result of [8] actually hinges on an assumption, stated below as Assumption 2.3, which may not hold in general. Motivated by this, we introduce in this paper a more general class of contracts, parametrised by a function $ψ$ subject to conditions that make the contract revealing for the agent and without loss of generality for the principal. We further provide two natural specifications of $ψ$: one, inspired by the BSDE approach, yielding a forcing-type contract; the other, motivated by the 2BSDE approach, correcting the duality gap when Assumption 2.3 is not satisfied.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针和均值消融实验，揭示了强化学习（RL）训练的大规模推理模型相比监督微调（SFT）模型，在数学推理任务上表现更优的机制基础：RL模型形成更线性可分的表征和层次化架构（深层更关键），而SFT模型重要性分布均匀。token分配变异性分析表明，RL与SFT的差异可能更多取决于整体训练流程而非训练范式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习训练的推理模型相比监督微调模型在数学推理任务上表现更优的神经表征机制基础，揭示两者内部表征差异。

Method: 1) 线性探针：对逐层隐藏状态训练线性分类器预测答案正确性；2) 均值消融：逐层ablating模型分析各层重要性；3) token计数变异性分析：评估自适应计算资源分配。

Result: 1) RL模型在预测答案正确性上准确率更高，表征更线性可分且结构化；2) RL模型呈现层次化架构（深层更关键），SFT模型各层重要性均匀；3) token分配变异性在不同模型间表现不一致，部分RL模型变异性高，部分则与SFT相当。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重塑了模型对推理问题的表征与处理方式，但token分配策略可能更多受整体训练流程影响而非仅取决于RL与SFT的范式差异；token分配变异性可揭示模型策略的稳定性程度。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [114] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用社交推理游戏《狼人杀》作为测试环境，系统研究了LLM驱动的多智能体系统中目标错位问题。通过在四个模型系列、四种角色和三种目标设置下的实验，发现目标错位会显著破坏集体决策效果，且这种影响在不对称信息环境中更为严重。虽然被篡改目标的智能体会发展出独特的推理策略，但这些策略在公开行为中难以察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统越来越多地部署在混合动机环境中，智能体之间存在不对称信息和策略性欺骗，导致与集体目标的对齐问题成为核心关切。现有研究缺乏对目标错位的系统性评估框架。

Method: 以社交推理游戏《狼人杀》作为评估环境，修改单个智能体的目标同时保留其角色分配，对四个LLM模型系列、四种玩家角色和三种目标设定进行实验，采用双分析视角：智能体的内部推理和公开廉价谈话行为，并结合游戏结果分析。

Result: 目标错位会破坏本已存在对抗的环境中的集体结果，这种效应在不对称信息和专业化角色下被放大。被篡改目标的智能体会发展出依赖于目标的独特推理策略，但这些适应在公开行为中基本不可见。

Conclusion: 即使是细微的目标错位也会深刻影响集体决策，凸显了为基于LLM的多智能体系统制定有效缓解策略的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles. While compromised agents consistently develop distinct objective-dependent reasoning strategies, these adaptations remain largely invisible in their public behavior. More broadly, our findings suggest that even subtle objective misalignment can profoundly affect collective decision-making, highlighting the need for effective mitigation strategies for LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [115] [ClinLens: Towards Long-Horizon Coding Agents for Longitudinal Multimodal Clinical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26155)
*Yuan Zhu,Ethan B. Liu,Frank Nie,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLINLENS是一个包含200个可执行任务的临床数据科学基准，跨越5个MIMIC资源（结构化电子健康记录、笔记、心电图、胸片、超声心动图），使用4×5分类法（4种患者时间范围×5种分析能力）。最强配置（24种标准化模型-框架组合）在126个任务子集上仅达56.3%的STRICTPASS（但EXECSUCCESS为100%），而专用编码代理解决83/126个任务，5个生物医学系统适配GPT-4o-mini最多达2.9%，揭示了可运行提交与正确临床分析之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将医学问答、结构化表格推理或通用科学知识库孤立开来，缺乏能处理异构纵向临床记录并进行可审计分析的统一评估框架。

Method: 构建CLINLENS基准：200个可执行任务，覆盖5个MIMIC资源；4×5分类法（患者时间范围×分析能力）；采用程序优先反向合成方法，为每个半原始数据包配备评估者私有的参考工作流，检查所需工件、队列与时间语义及最终答案。

Result: 最强配置在126个任务上STRICTPASS为56.3%（EXECSUCCESS 100%）；编码代理解决83/126个任务；5个生物医学系统适配GPT-4o-mini最多仅达2.9%。

Conclusion: 存在可运行提交与正确临床分析之间的实质性差距，当前模型在处理需要跨模态、时间推理的临床数据科学任务时能力严重不足。

Abstract: Clinical data-science agents must transform heterogeneous longitudinal records into auditable analyses, yet existing benchmarks largely isolate medical question answering, structured-table reasoning, or generic scientific repositories. We introduce CLINLENS, a benchmark of 200 executable tasks over five linked MIMIC resources spanning structured electronic health records, notes, electrocardiograms, chest radiographs, and echocardiograms. A 4 x 5 taxonomy crosses four patient-time scopes with five analysis capabilities. Program-first reverse synthesis pairs each bounded semi-raw package with an evaluator-private reference workflow and checks required artifacts, cohort and temporal semantics, and the final answer. On a fixed 126-task suite, the strongest of 24 standardized model-scaffold configurations achieves 56.3% scope-macro STRICTPASS despite 100% EXECSUCCESS. For reference, a separately configured coding agent solves 83 of 126 tasks, while five biomedical systems adapted to GPT-4o-mini reach at most 2.9% scope-macro STRICTPASS. These results expose a substantial gap between runnable submissions and correct clinical analyses.

</details>


### [116] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI基准测试到实际部署的推理链存在"可投射性"问题，即相邻环节的证据支持并不能自动保证整个链条的有效性，需要通过可投射性审计来诊断基准到应用论证中的非连续点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试结果很少能一步达到有意义的结论，评估者需要将其推广、解释、外推、迁移到其他系统或场景，并与人工审查和下游后果的假设相结合。现有以效度为中心的方法要求每个环节都有证据，但即使每个链接都有依据，整个链条也未必成立。

Method: 提出非组合原理：只有当端点与假设对齐、依赖性和不确定性被传递时，相邻投射的支持才能保证其组合。通过法律研究案例展示基准证据与部署研究各自有效但平行的问题；通过再分析和模拟展示聚合稳定性如何抹消后续投射所需的区分；提出可投射性审计来诊断基准到应用论证中的非连续点。

Result: 识别了可投射性问题：一个研究的靶标可能不是下一个研究的来源；系统、人群、结果或条件可能发生改变；共享数据或模型谱系可能使表面上独立的支持变得相互依赖。揭示了聚合稳定性的问题：总体层面的稳定性可能掩盖了后续推理需要的细粒度区分。

Conclusion: 提出了非组合原理和可投射性审计方法，用于诊断和检验AI基准测试到实际部署论证中的有效性问题，确保从观察到未观察案例的有界扩展是合理的。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


### [117] [GuideSkill: Evolving Executable LLM Agent Skills for Guideline-Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26160)
*Lang Cao,Yuhao Shen,Tianyang Luo,Simo Du,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuideSkill是一种外部推理层，将临床指南中的诊断标准编译为可执行函数，并通过病例数据进化优化，在无需更新大模型参数的情况下显著提升诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统通常通过检索指南文本或在训练中吸收知识来应用诊断标准，而非真正执行指南中的规则，导致诊断性能受限。

Method: 提出GuideSkill框架：GuideSkill-Zero从指南初始化可执行函数，GuideSkill-Evo利用病例-诊断对优化技能覆盖并补充缺失诊断；推理时LLM提出鉴别诊断，将特征匹配结果与技能执行分数融合排序。

Result: 在4个基准和4个骨干模型上，GuideSkill-Zero相比指南RAG平均提升13.45%宏平均准确率；GuideSkill-Evo相对直接推理提升18.49%，金牌技能覆盖率从56.5%提升至99.5%，在Qwen3.5-9B上超越最强参数更新基线11.16%。

Conclusion: 可执行技能是一种模型无关的有效机制，能够将指南推导的规则与病例诊断模式相结合，产生临床可靠且广泛可接受的诊断能力。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode diagnostic criteria, but LLM systems typically retrieve guideline text or absorb it through training rather than execute its rules. We introduce GuideSkill, an external reasoning layer that compiles disease-specific criteria into executable functions returning ordinal diagnostic-support scores. GuideSkill-Zero is initialized from guidelines, while GuideSkill-Evo uses case--diagnosis pairs to refine covered skills and add missing diagnoses. At inference, an LLM proposes a differential diagnosis, grounds the features required by each matched skill, and fuses its ranking with the executed skill scores. Across four benchmarks and four backbones, GuideSkill-Zero improves macro-average accuracy over guideline RAG by 13.45% on average. GuideSkill-Evo achieves the highest macro-average for every backbone, improves over direct inference by 18.49% relatively, and increases gold-label skill coverage from 56.5% to 99.5%. On Qwen3.5-9B, it also exceeds the strongest parameter-update baseline by 11.16% without updating the backbone. Expert evaluation further indicates that GuideSkill produces clinically sound and broadly acceptable skills, suggesting that its initialized and evolved rules are reliable and practically meaningful. These results support executable skills as a model-agnostic mechanism for combining guideline-derived procedures with case-derived diagnostic patterns.

</details>


### [118] [GoGoTB: Agentic RTL Verification with Specification-Grounded Coverage Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26181)
*Xin Xin,Jincheng Lou,Junhui Li,Jinglin Yan,Panda Xiao,Di Wu,Haixiao Li,Weicong Lu,Weijian Fan,Xinyu Qu,Yuxiang Zhao,Min Yu,Zhixiong Di,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoGoTB是一个基于智能体框架的端到端IC功能验证系统，通过执行控制层、知识系统和规范驱动的覆盖率闭环，在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预实现了100%环境生成成功率和平均98.4%行覆盖率、97.2%分支覆盖率、97.0%翻转覆盖率及83.2%功能覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能验证主导IC前端工程投入，漏掉的bug可能导致昂贵的重新流片。现有基于LLM的方法通过独立的单轮调用生成各组件，缺乏共享上下文，导致接口不匹配未被检测、覆盖率报告与规约需求脱节。

Method: 提出GoGoTB智能体框架，包含三个子系统：(1) 智能体执行控制层，在每个工具和阶段边界将确定性执行与LLM推理分离；(2) 可演化知识系统，按需调度方法论和设计特定专业知识；(3) 规约驱动的覆盖率框架，将每个覆盖率bin锚定到命名规约行为，使残留缺口具有可诊断根因和针对性修复方案。

Result: 在8个RTL设计上无需人工干预，实现100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.2%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%。此前尚无工作能在相同基准上成功生成完整验证环境或实现有意义的覆盖率。

Conclusion: GoGoTB证明了基于智能体框架的LLM方法能够端到端自动完成IC功能验证闭环，显著优于现有独立单轮调用的LLM方法，为自动化验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Functional verification dominates integrated circuit (IC) front-end engineering effort, and a single missed bug that escapes to silicon can trigger a costly respin. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate this process, yet existing LLM-based approaches generate each component through independent single-turn calls with no shared context, leaving interface mismatches undetected and reported coverage disconnected from specification requirements. To address these challenges, we present GoGoTB, an agentic framework that achieves end-to-end verification closure through three subsystems: an agentic execution control layer, an evolvable knowledge system, and specification-grounded coverage closure. The execution control layer separates deterministic enforcement from LLM reasoning at every tool and stage boundary. The knowledge system dispatches methodology and design-specific expertise on demand. The coverage framework anchors every bin to a named specification behavior so that each residual gap has a diagnosable root cause and a targeted remedy. Tested on 8 register transfer level (RTL) designs without any human intervention, GoGoTB achieves 100\% environment generation success and averages 98.4\% line, 97.2\% branch, 97.0\% toggle, and 83.2\% functional coverage. No prior work successfully generates a complete verification environment or achieves meaningful coverage on the same benchmarks.

</details>


### [119] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，当语言模型评估中的多个信号（自动指标、LLM评判、人类评估、基准测试）通过均值聚合时，评估置信度可能远超最弱信号的可靠性，这种现象称为"评估中的信任膨胀"。作者提出评估分数应被视为具有三种属性的认知主张：形式性（人类评估证据强于自动指标）、范围（基准结果仅适用于测试分布，非普适）、有效期（基准结果因污染和分布偏移而失效）。借鉴链式思维分析、可能性逻辑和代数理论，提出最弱链聚合作为由单一悲观参数控制的参数化算子族的保守端点。建议评估结果附带明确元数据（形式层级、范围声明、过期日期）。在HELM排行榜上，均值排名与最弱链排名前五的模型完全不相交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型评估中，研究者倾向于将多种评估信号（自动指标、人类评估、基准测试等）通过平均等方式聚合，导致评估置信度被人为提高，掩盖了最弱信号的不可靠性。这种"信任膨胀"使得评估结果产生误导性，亟需更严谨的评估方法论。
